  "content": "# Spec: X Viral AI-Demo Discovery via a Free Search API (CI-Only)\n\n**Short name:** `x-search-discovery`\n**Status:** Draft spec (no code changes made)\n**Related:** `src/viral_finder.py`, `.github/workflows/find-viral-x-links.yml`,\n`docs/x-browser-discovery.md`\n\n---\n\n## 1. Origin of this request\n\nThe requester reported that a **plain logged-out (\"incognito\") browser search**\nworked for them: they searched a topic in simple words on **google.com**, a list\nof links came back, and when they clicked through to the x.com posts they could\nread the metadata clearly **without logging in** — view count, timestamp/date,\nplayable video, post text, author handle, and likes/replies/reposts.\n\nThe existing implementation concluded that discovery was infeasible for $0 and\nfell back to a hybrid. The requester's observation contradicts that conclusion\nfor **their** environment, so this spec re-derives discovery around a real,\nreproducible search path.\n\n### 1.1 Measured evidence (verified in this repository's Codespace)\n\n| Probe | Result |\n| :--- | :--- |\n| `x.com/search?q=...` (live + top) in headless Chromium, logged out | **HTTP 403, \"Just a moment…\"** (Cloudflare challenge) |\n| `mobile.x.com/search?q=...` | **HTTP 403** |\n| `x.com/<user>/status/<id>` rendered page | **HTTP 403** |\n| `x.com/explore` | **HTTP 403** |\n| `google.com/search` (HTTP) for `site:x.com …` and natural-language queries | HTTP 200 but a **JS-gated** page (`enablejs`) with **0** `x.com/*/status/*` links |\n| `bing.com/search` `site:x.com …` | HTTP 200, generic AI landing pages, **0** status links |\n| `html.duckduckgo.com` / `lite.duckduckgo.com` | **HTTP 202 anti-bot \"anomaly\"** page |\n| Reddit public `search.json` | **HTTP 403** |\n| `cdn.syndication.twimg.com/tweet-result?id=…` | **HTTP 200**, anonymous: text, `created_at`, media `type: video` (no view count) |\n| `yt-dlp` on a real public post | Verified **view_count=34,496,153**, **timestamp**, native video, and CDN answered a ranged `206 video/mp4` |\n\n**Interpretation:** the requester's working path depends on a **residential IP +\nreal browser** (Google serves real results there, and x.com serves the logged-out\npage). From a GitHub-hosted (datacenter) runner neither happens. Therefore a\nbrowser-only CI approach cannot reproduce the requester's experience; a\n**free-tier search API** can, because it returns Google-quality results over\nHTTPS without needing a browser or a residential IP.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Goal\n\nBuild a **single, unified, CI-only pipeline** that:\n\n1. **Discovers** candidate public X post URLs using a free search API (**Exa**),\n2. **Verifies** each candidate using anonymous, permitted metadata endpoints,\n3. Writes only **genuinely qualifying** records into the repository, and\n4. Is honest — it never fabricates metadata and reports nothing found when\n   nothing qualifies.\n\n### Non-goals\n\n- No browser automation is required for the primary path (see §9 for the deferred\n  browser decision).\n- No downloading of videos (downloading stays a separate, deliberate action via\n  the existing `Download X Video` workflow).\n- No scraping of rendered x.com pages when they are blocked.\n- No bypassing CAPTCHAs, login gates, Cloudflare challenges, rate limits, or\n  other access controls — ever.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Hard constraints (non-negotiable)\n\n- **Total budget $0.** No paid APIs, subscriptions, or overage charges.\n- **No credit card, ever.** The search provider must not require a payment\n  method to obtain and use its free tier. *(Exa's free tier states no payment\n  method required — this must be re-confirmed before implementation and\n  re-checked in the spec review.)*\n- **No credentials of the requester's own browser session** committed to GitHub\n  Actions. No cookies, no `auth_token`, no session state.\n- **No fabricated data.** Missing/unverifiable fields → the post is excluded.\n- **CI-only.** The pipeline must run on GitHub-hosted `ubuntu-latest` with\n  `workflow_dispatch`; it must not require the requester's local machine.\n- **Preserve existing working functionality** (the downloader, its tests, the\n  `Download X Video` workflow).\n- **Keep qualifying thresholds exactly**: **≥ 50,000 views** and a **rolling\n  24-hour** publication window. (These become inputs with those exact defaults;\n  they must not be silently loosened.)\n\n---\n\n## 4. Discovery design\n\n### 4.1 Primary provider: Exa\n\n- **Why Exa:** free tier with **$10/month in credits (~2,800 searches)** and\n  **no payment method required**, semantic web search that reliably surfaces\n  specific URL types (social post links), and an HTTPS API usable from CI.\n- **Auth:** `EXA_API_KEY` stored as a **GitHub repository secret**, injected as\n  an environment variable at run time, never echoed to logs, never committed.\n\n### 4.2 Provider abstraction\n\nDefine a small internal interface (e.g. `SearchProvider.search(query, *,\nnum_results, include_domains) -> list[SearchHit]`) so Exa is the default but a\nsecond provider can be added later without touching the pipeline. This keeps the\n\"free-tier provider\" decision reversible.\n\n### 4.3 Query templates (use a mix)\n\nSeveral templates are run per execution, combining the styles the requester\nselected:\n\n1. **Semantic**, no operator — e.g. `frontier model release demo video`,\n   `computer use agent demo`, `built with <model> demo`.\n2. **Domain-filtered** — the same phrases with\n   `include_domains=[\"x.com\", \"twitter.com\"]`.\n3. **Literal operator** — `site:x.com <topic> demo video` inside the query\n   string, for providers/engines that honor it.\n\nTemplates must be data-driven (a list in config) so they can be tuned without\ncode changes.\n\n### 4.4 Seed topics (default set)\n\n- New **frontier model releases** (GPT/Gemini/Claude/Llama/etc. demos).\n- **Computer-use / browser agents**.\n- **Anything made by / demonstrated with a frontier model** (generative video,\n  images, code, agents, robotics) — treated as the broad umbrella.\n\n(Topics are inputs, so the set can be extended per run.)\n\n### 4.5 Query budget & cost guardrails\n\n- Requester preference: **use as many queries as the free cap allows.**\n- Still enforce guardrails so one run cannot silently burn the whole month:\n  - a configurable **per-run query cap** with a safe default (derived from the\n    monthly free allowance ÷ expected runs),\n  - **stop early** if the provider reports the cap is exhausted,\n  - log **queries used / queries remaining** in the run summary,\n  - never proceed into paid/overage usage.\n\n### 4.6 Result handling\n\n- Extract **only** URLs that parse to a numeric post id; **discard non-status\n  hits** (profiles/homepages).\n- Log a **hit-rate statistic** (total results vs. status-URL results) so query\n  templates can be tuned over time.\n- Normalize and **de-duplicate by post id** before verification.\n\n### 4.7 Fallback discovery (when no key, or API unavailable/exhausted)\n\nRequester preference is to **continue with what still works**:\n\n1. Explicit **`candidate_urls`** input (author-provided public X URLs).\n2. The existing **Hacker News / Algolia** best-effort source (keep it).\n3. If neither yields candidates, exit cleanly with a clear \"no candidates\"\n   message (no empty file, no empty commit).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Verification design\n\nMetadata source precedence (requester-selected):\n\n1. **Primary — anonymous metadata endpoints.** `yt-dlp`'s public Twitter\n   extractor (view count, publication timestamp, native video, formats) plus\n   `cdn.syndication.twimg.com` as an independent cross-check for text /\n   `created_at` / media type. Both work from CI with no credentials.\n2. **Cross-check — rendered post page when accessible.** If the page loads\n   (it usually will not in CI), cross-check; if it returns 403, **do not\n   bypass** and do not treat the failure as a hard error.\n3. **Never fabricate.** Any field that cannot be verified from a permitted\n   source marks the record **unverified** and **excludes** it from results.\n\n### 5.1 Per-candidate checks (all must pass)\n\n| Check | Rule |\n| :--- | :--- |\n| URL valid & public | Normalizes to `https://x.com/<user>/status/<id>`; post is reachable anonymously |\n| Publication time | Obtained from real metadata; within the rolling **24h** window vs actual execution time (small clock-skew tolerance) |\n| Views | Real observed count **≥ 50,000** |\n| AI relevance | Post text/description references a frontier model, agent, or capability (word-boundary matching; not merely mentioning \"AI\") |\n| Video present | A genuine native video stream is present (not image/text-only) |\n| Video accessible | Stream is publicly fetchable (verified via a ranged GET; only a few KB fetched — never the whole file) |\n\n### 5.2 Cross-checking policy\n\n- Text/date/video: confirm via syndication where possible.\n- Views: only from the anonymous GraphQL/yt-dlp path (syndication does not expose\n  view counts).\n- Disagreement between sources → treat the field as unverified → exclude.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Pipeline architecture (unified, single workflow)\n\nRequester chose to **unify into one pipeline**, replacing the current discovery\nsources with Exa while keeping the existing verification engine:\n\n```\nExa search (templates) ─┐\ncandidate_urls input ───┼─▶ normalize ─▶ dedupe by post_id ─▶ verify\nHN/Algolia (fallback) ──┘                                        │\n                                                                 ▼\n                          viral-links/YYYY-MM-DD.json ◀── filter (all 6 checks)\n                                                                 │\n                                                       prune old files ─▶ commit\n```\n\n- One workflow, `workflow_dispatch` only (**no cron schedule**).\n- One CLI entrypoint the workflow calls.\n- Verification, filtering, schema, and de-duplication reuse the existing\n  `src/viral_finder.py` behavior; only the **discovery source** changes.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Output & storage\n\n- **Stored in the repo itself**, committed by the workflow (requester: \"I want it\n  stored in the repo itself\").\n- Path: **`viral-links/YYYY-MM-DD.json`** (UTC execution date).\n- Top-level `generated_at` (ISO 8601, tz-aware) plus a `records` array.\n- Record fields (unchanged): `post_url`, `post_id`, `post_text`, `published_at`,\n  `views`, `model_or_topic`, `video_present`, `video_accessible`,\n  `verification_status`, `evidence_source`, `discovered_at`.\n- **Dedup** by `post_id` across runs; repeated runs merge rather than duplicate.\n- **Retention:** keep daily files, **prune older than N days**, **default 30**,\n  **configurable** via a workflow input. Pruning commits the deletions.\n- **No file and no commit** when there are zero qualifying records.\n- Downloaded video binaries are never committed by this workflow.\n\n---\n\n## 8. GitHub Actions workflow spec\n\n- **Trigger:** `workflow_dispatch` only. No `schedule`.\n- **Runner:** `ubuntu-latest`, headless.\n- **Permissions:** `contents: write` only (needed to commit results/prune).\n- **Inputs (validated):**\n  - `query` / `topics` (default: the §4.4 seed set)\n  - `candidate_urls` (optional, multiline)\n  - `max_candidates` (default 25)\n  - `min_views` (**default 50,000**)\n  - `hours` (**default 24**)\n  - `retention_days` (**default 30**)\n  - `per_run_query_cap` (default derived from the free allowance)\n  - `discover_exa` / `discover_hn` toggles\n- **Secrets:** `EXA_API_KEY` (repo secret). Never logged. Missing/exhausted key →\n  **fall back** to `candidate_urls` + HN and continue (§4.7).\n- **Failure behavior:**\n  - Required capability genuinely unavailable (e.g. network-blocked metadata) →\n    fail clearly with a distinct exit code/message.\n  - Blocked by Cloudflare/403 on page cross-check → proceed via anonymous\n    endpoints; not an error.\n  - Rate limit (HTTP 429) from the provider → bounded exponential backoff, then\n    stop gracefully (no aggressive retry loops).\n  - Zero qualifying records → success with an explicit \"no qualifying records\"\n    summary; no file, no commit.\n- **Run summary:** queries run, queries/hit-rate, candidates found, verified,\n  excluded-with-reasons, retention pruned, committed y/n.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Browser automation (deferred decision)\n\nRequester picked \"you recommend, I decide later\" for the browser tool.\n\n**Recommendation:** **do not add browser automation to the primary path.** Exa\n(discovery) + anonymous metadata endpoints (verification) meet the goal without a\nbrowser, and every browser path tested in CI is Cloudflare/JS blocked. If a\nbrowser is later required, use **Playwright (Python)** (already present, Python-\nnative) and document that it only works on a non-datacenter IP; it must never\nattempt to solve/defeat a challenge.\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing requirements\n\nUnit tests (offline, deterministic) must cover:\n\n- Post-URL normalization and post-id extraction (aliases, `/i/web/status`,\n  `/statuses/`, trailing segments, query strings, HTML-escaped `&amp;`).\n- De-duplication by post id (in-memory and on-disk/merge).\n- Rolling 24h filtering (incl. boundary, future timestamps, naive timestamps).\n- The 50,000-view threshold and configurable alternatives.\n- AI relevance filtering (positives + non-obvious negatives like \"email\").\n- Video presence and verification status.\n- Missing/invalid metadata → excluded (no fabrication).\n- JSON schema and tz-aware ISO 8601 timestamp validation.\n- Zero qualifying results → no file written, no commit.\n- Existing downloader regressions.\n- New: search-result handling (non-status hits discarded; hit-rate logging).\n- New: fallback path when the key is absent/exhausted.\n\nIntegration tests (real network):\n\n- **Real Exa integration test gated on `EXA_API_KEY`.** If the secret is absent,\n  the test must report **blocked/skipped** and must never be counted as passing.\n- Real `yt-dlp` metadata extraction + ranged video accessibility on a public post.\n- Real HN source reachability.\n- Documented finding that datacenter search engines surface no status links.\n\n---\n\n## 11. Security & privacy\n\n- `EXA_API_KEY` only via GitHub secret → env var; never printed, never committed.\n- No cookies, tokens, or session data from the requester's browser enter CI.\n- Tests must not require secrets to run the offline suite.\n- No logging of secrets or full request headers.\n\n---\n\n## 12. Edge cases to handle explicitly\n\n- Exa returns profiles/homepages, not `/status/` URLs → discard, count for tuning.\n- Exa returns duplicate URLs across templates → dedupe by post id.\n- Post is text-only or image-only → excluded (\"no genuine video\").\n- Post is deleted / protected / age-gated → excluded (not \"verified false\").\n- Both `twitter.com` and `x.com` (and `mobile.`/`www.`) variants → same id.\n- `/i/web/status/<id>` links → normalized.\n- HTML-escaped `&amp;` in URLs from search snippets → unescaped.\n- Clock skew: post timestamp slightly in the future → excluded unless within a\n  small tolerance.\n- Provider returns 429 or the monthly cap is hit → backoff/stop, log remaining.\n- Missing `EXA_API_KEY` → fallback discovery, still produce verified records from\n  `candidate_urls`.\n- Empty results on a run → no file, no commit, honest summary.\n\n---\n\n## 13. Acceptance criteria\n\n1. A single `workflow_dispatch` workflow (no cron) runs on `ubuntu-latest` and\n   performs Exa discovery → verification → commit-to-repo in one pass.\n2. `EXA_API_KEY` is used only from a repo secret; missing/exhausted → falls back\n   to `candidate_urls` + HN without failing silently.\n3. Qualifying defaults remain **≥ 50,000 views** and **rolling 24h**.\n4. Results are committed to `viral-links/YYYY-MM-DD.json`, deduped by `post_id`,\n   with older files pruned past the configurable retention (default 30 days).\n5. Records contain all required fields; timestamps are tz-aware ISO 8601; no\n   fabricated values.\n6. Posts failing any check (views, window, AI relevance, video, accessibility,\n   verifiability) are excluded with a logged reason.\n7. Zero qualifying records → no file and no commit; a clear summary is printed.\n8. The offline unit suite passes; the real Exa integration test is present and\n   correctly reports **blocked** when the secret is absent.\n9. Existing downloader and its tests remain intact and passing.\n\n---\n\n## 14. Decisions already made (do not re-litigate)\n\n- CI-only; no local machine required.\n- Discovery provider: **Exa** (hard no-card constraint).\n- Query style: **mix of templates**; seed topics = frontier model releases,\n  computer-use/browser agents, anything made by a frontier model.\n- Verification: **anonymous endpoints first**; page cross-check when accessible;\n  exclude on unverifiable; never fabricate/bypass.\n- One unified pipeline; auto-commit verified records (no human approval gate).\n- Results live in the repo; 50k/24h unchanged; retention configurable (30d).\n\n## 15. Open questions (resolve during implementation)\n\n- Confirm Exa's free tier still requires **no payment method** at signup.\n- Exact default `per_run_query_cap` and how to track remaining monthly credits\n  (does Exa's API/dashboard expose balance programmatically?).\n- Whether the HN fallback stays enabled by default now that Exa is primary.\n- Final query-template wording and how many templates per run by default.\n"
}
````

    Create a key in the dashboard. New accounts start with free credits.
    Authorizations
    apiKeybearer
    
    x-api-key
    string
    header
    required
    Pass your Exa API key in the x-api-key header. You can also authenticate with Authorization: Bearer
    .
    Body
    application/json
    
    query
    string
    required
    The query string for the search.
    Minimum string length: 1
    
    includeDomains
    string[] | null
    List of domains or domain paths to include in the search. Each entry can be a hostname (for example, example.com), a hostname with a path prefix (for example, example.com/docs), or a wildcard subdomain (for example, *.example.com). If specified, results will only come from the matching domains or paths. Use this parameter for domain or path filtering instead of adding a site: operator to the query.
    Maximum array length: 1200
    
    excludeDomains
    string[] | null
    List of domains or domain paths to exclude from search results. Each entry can be a hostname (for example, example.com), a hostname with a path prefix (for example, example.com/docs), or a wildcard subdomain (for example, *.example.com). If specified, no results will be returned from the matching domains or paths. Use this parameter for domain or path filtering instead of adding a site: operator to the query.
    Maximum array length: 1200
    
    startPublishedDate
    string<date-time> | null
    Only links with a published date after this will be returned. Must be specified in ISO 8601 format.
    
    endPublishedDate
    string<date-time> | null
    Only links with a published date before this will be returned. Must be specified in ISO 8601 format.
    
    numResults
    integer | null
    default:10
    Number of results to return. Limits vary by search type. The maximum public limit is 100 results. Contact sales (hello@exa.ai) to discuss higher limits.
    Required range
    : 1 <= x <= 100
    
    moderation
    boolean | null
    default:false
    Enable content moderation to filter unsafe content from search results.
    
    contents
    object | null
    Content options for text, highlights, summary, extras, and freshness controls.
    Show
    child attributes
    
    additionalQueries
    string[] | null
    Additional query variations for deep-search variants. Only works with a deep-search type. When provided, these queries are used alongside the main query for broader results.
    Required array length: 1 - 10 element
    s
    
    type
    enum<string> | null
    default:auto
    The search mode to use. auto (default) is a balanced mode that optimizes for both quality and speed and is recommended for most applications. fast returns high-quality results with reduced latency, making it a good fit for user-facing search and interactive workflows. instant is optimized for minimum response time, trading some search depth for speed in real-time experiences such as chat, voice agents, and autocomplete. deep-lite performs lightweight research with synthesized results and a consistent 4-second latency, lower than full deep search. deep conducts comprehensive multi-step research with synthesis, while deep-reasoning adds stronger reasoning for complex analysis and decision-making tasks.
    Available options
    :
    instant,
    fast,
    auto,
    deep-lite,
    deep,
    deep-reasoning
    
    category
    enum<string> | null
    A data category to focus on. Known categories include company, publication, news, personal site, financial report, and people. Other strings are accepted and used as category hints for search. The people and company categories have improved quality for finding people profiles and company pages. The publication category surfaces scholarly publications such as research papers, preprints, and journal articles, with structured metadata like authors, venue, and citations. Note: The company and people categories only support a limited set of filters. The following parameters are NOT supported for these categories: startPublishedDate, endPublishedDate, excludeDomains. Using unsupported parameters will result in a 400 error.
    Available options
    :
    company,
    publication,
    news,
    personal site,
    financial report,
    people
    
    userLocation
    Option 1 · stringOption 2 · objectOption 3 · object
    The end user's location, used to localize results. Either a two-letter ISO 3166-1 country code string (e.g. US) or an object with country and/or latitude + longitude in decimal degrees. Coordinates are optional and must be sent together; country alone is equivalent to the string form. Send only the precision your use case needs.
    
    compliance
    enum<string> | null
    Enterprise-only compliance mode. Set to hipaa for HIPAA mode. Requires cache-only retrieval with supported parameters. See the HIPAA docs for details.
    Available options
    :
    hipaa
    
    outputSchema
    object
    JSON schema for synthesized output. Supported root types are "text" and "object". When provided, the response includes an output object whose content matches this schema. Works with every search type and adds about 2 seconds of synthesis latency on top of the selected search type. Object schemas are limited to 10 properties in total (nested and array items properties count toward the limit) and 2 levels of nesting, and every array must define items.
    Option 1
    Option 2
    Show
    child attributes
    
    objective
    string | null
    The broader goal this search serves: the task the caller is working on, beyond the query itself. When exposing this field to an agent as a tool parameter, describe it as: "Goal for this search turn; say which documents should rank first, which should be excluded, and what specific facts or figures to pull from them."
    Maximum string length: 4096
    
    userIdentifier
    string | null
    An opaque identifier your platform generates for the end user this request is made on behalf of, such as a UUID. Exa records it with the request so that usage and abuse can be attributed to one end user instead of your whole team. It must not be or contain personal information such as an email address, phone number, usernam

> Prune function:
> ```python
> def prune_old_results(output_dir, retention_days, now=None) -> List[Path]:
>     now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
>     if retention_days <= 0: return []
>     if not output_dir.exists(): return []
>     cutoff = (now.astimezone(timezone.utc).date() - timedelta(days=retention_days))
>     removed=[]
>     for path in sorted(output_dir.glob("*.json")):
>         m = re.fullmatch(r"(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})\.json", path.name)
>         if not m: continue
>         try: date = datetime.strptime(m.group(1), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
>         except ValueError: continue
>         if date < cutoff:
>             path.unlink(); removed.append(path)
>     return removed
> ```

  "content": "\"\"\"Free-tier web search provider (Exa) for X-post discovery.\n\nExa's `/search` endpoint is a plain HTTPS API that needs only an ``x-api-key``\nheader, so it works from GitHub-hosted CI where browser search and direct\n``x.com`` page access are Cloudflare/JS blocked. The free tier requires no\npayment method and its credits refresh monthly.\n\nThis module defines:\n\n* :class:`SearchProvider` — a tiny protocol so Exa is the default but any other\n  provider could be swapped in without touching the pipeline.\n* :class:`ExaSearchProvider` — an ``urllib``-based client (no extra dependency).\n* Query templates derived from seed topics, mixing semantic phrases, a domain\n  filter, and the literal ``site:x.com`` operator.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport time\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\nfrom typing import Callable, List, Optional, Protocol, Sequence, Tuple\n\nEXA_SEARCH_URL = \"https://api.exa.ai/search\"\nDEFAULT_EXA_SEARCH_TYPE = \"auto\"\nDEFAULT_EXA_NUM_RESULTS = 10\n# Per-run number of Exa queries. agent demo video\",\n    \"AI video generation model demo\",\n    \"multimodal AI model demonstration\",\n    \"humanoid robot AI demonstration\",\n)\n\n\nclass SearchProviderError(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Base error for search-provider failures.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass SearchProviderAuthError(SearchProviderError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when the provider rejects or is missing credentials.\"\"\"\n\n\nclass \"\"\"Raised when the provider reports the free allowance/credits are used up.\"\"\"\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass SearchHit:\n    \"\"\"A single search result: a URL plus whatever context the provider gives.\"\"\"\n\n    url: str\n    title: str = \"\"\n    published_date: Optional[str] = None\n    provider: str = \"exa\"\n\n\nclass SearchProvider(Protocol):\n    \"\"\"Minimal interface # pragma: no cover - protocol\n        ...\n\n\ndef _safe_error_body(exc: urllib.error.HTTPError) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Reads a short, non-sensitive snippet of an error response body.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        raw = exc.read()\n    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - best effort only\n        return \"\"\n    if isinstance(raw, bytes):\n        return raw.decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")[:200]\n    return str(raw)[:200]\n\n\nclass ExaSearchProvider:\n    \"\"\"Exa ``/search`` client over ``urllib`` with bounded backoff.\"\"\"\n\n    name = \"exa\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        api_key: Optional[str],\n        *,\n        search_type: str = DEFAULT_EXA_SEARCH_TYPE,\n        timeout: int = 30,\n        max_retries: int = 3,\n        backoff_seconds: float = 2.0,\n        opener: Callable[..., object] = urllib.request.urlopen,\n        sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._api_key = (api_key or \"\").strip()\n        self.search_type = search_type\n        self.timeout = timeout\n        self.max_retries = max(0, int(max_retries))\n        self.backoff_seconds = max(0.0, float(backoff_seconds))\n        self._opener = opener\n        self._sleep = sleep\n        self.queries_used = 0\n        self.total_cost_usd = 0.0\n\n    def is_configured(self) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"True when an API key is present.\"\"\"\n        return bool(self._api_key)\n\n    def search(\n        self,\n        query: str,\n        *,\n        num_results: int = DEFAULT_EXA_NUM_RESULTS,\n        include_domains: Optional[Sequence[str]] = None,\n        start_published_date: Optional[str] = None,\n    ) -> List[SearchHit]:\n        \"\"\"Runs one Exa search and returns normalised hits.\n\n        Raises:\n            SearchProviderAuthError: missing/invalid key (HTTP 401/403).\n            SearchProviderExhaustedError: free allowance used up (HTTP 402).\n            SearchProviderRateLimitError: still rate limited after retries (429).\n            SearchProviderError: any other request/parse failure.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self.is_configured():\n            raise SearchProviderAuthError(\"EXA_API_KEY is not set\")\n\n        count = max(1, min(int(num_results), 100))\n        payload: dict = {\n            \"query\": query,\n            \"type\": self.search_type,\n            \"numResults\": count,\n        }\n        if include_domains:\n            payload[\"includeDomains\"] = list(include_domains)\n        if start_published_date:\n    headers={\n                \"x-api-key\": self._api_key,\n                \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\",\n                \"Accept\": \"application/json\",\n                \"User-Agent\": \"viral-x-finder/1.0 (+free-tier)\",\n            },\n            method=\"POST\",\n        )\n\n        body: Optional[str] = None\n        attempt = 0\n        while attempt <= self.max_retries:\n            try:\n                with self._opener(request, timeout=self.timeout) as response:\n                    body = response.read().decode(\"utf-8\", \"replace\")\n                break\n            except urllib.error.HTTPError as exc:\n                status = exc.code\n                detail = _safe_error_body(exc)\n                if status in (401, 403):\n                    raise SearchProviderAuthError(\n                        f\"Exa rejected the API key (HTTP {status}): {detail}\"\n                    ) from exc\n                if status == 402:\n                    raise SearchProviderExhaustedError(\n                        f\"Exa free allowance exhausted (HTTP {status}): {detail}\"\n                    ) from exc\n                if status == 429:\n self.max_retries:\n                    self._sleep(self.backoff_seconds * (2**attempt))\n                    attempt += 1\n                    continue\n                raise SearchProviderError(f\"Exa request failed: {exc}\") from exc\n\n        if body is None:  # pragma: no cover - defensive\n            raise SearchProviderError(\"Exa request produced no response\")\n\n        try:\n            parsed = json.loads(body)\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:\n            raise SearchProviderError(f\"Exa returned invalid JSON: {exc}\") from url = item.get(\"url\")\n            if not url:\n                continue\n            hits.append(\n                SearchHit(\n                    url=str(url),\n                    title=str(item.get(\"title\") or \"\"),\n                    published_date=item.get(\"publishedDate\"),\n                    provider=self.name,\n                )\n            )\n        return hits\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass QueryTemplate:\n    \"\"\"A single discovery query plus the domain filter it should use.\"\"\"\n\n    label: str\n    query: str\n    include_domains: Tuple[str, ...] = ()\n\n\ndef build_query_templates(\n    topics: Sequence[str] = DEFAULT_TOPICS,\n    *,\n    site_operator: bool = True,\n) -> List[QueryTemplate]:\n    \"\"\"Builds a mix of semantic, domain-filtered, and ``site:`` query templates.\"\"\"\n    templates: List[QueryTemplate] = []\n    for topic in topics:\n        clean = topic.strip()\n        if not clean:\n            continue\n        templates.append(QueryTemplate(label=f\"semantic:{clean}\", query=clean))\n        templates.append(\n            QueryTemplate(\n                label=f\"domain:{clean}\",\n                query=clean,\n                include_domains=X_DOMAINS,\n            )\n        )\n        if site_operator:\n            templates.append(\n                QueryTemplate(label=f\"site:{clean}\", query=f\"site:x.com {clean}\")\n            )\n    return templates\n"

>     results_total: int = 0
>     status_hits: int = 0

> def discover_with_exa(
>     provider: "SearchProvider",

>     log: Callable[[str], None] = print,

>

> 
>     if not provider.is_configured():

>                 num_results=num_results,

>             )
>         except SearchProviderExhaustedError as exc:

>         except SearchProviderRateLimitError as exc:
>             stats.notes.append(f"rate limited: {exc}")
>             log(f"[WARN] Exa rate limited: {exc}")
>             break
>         except SearchProviderError as exc:
>             stats.notes.append(f"{template.label}: {exc}")
>             log(f"[WARN] Exa query failed for {template.label}: {exc}")
>             continue

>         stats.results_total += len(hits)

>         f"{stats.non_status_discarded} non-status discarded, "
>         f"hit-rate {stats.status_hit_rate:.0%}."
>     )

> def prune_old_results(output_dir: Path, retention_days: int, now: Optional[datetime] = None) -> List[Path]:
>     """Deletes daily result files older than ``retention_days`` (UTC)."""
>     if retention_days <= 0:
>         return []
>     now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
>     if not output_dir.exists():
>         return []
>     cutoff = now.astimezone(timezone.utc).date() - timedelta(days=retention_days)
>     removed: List[Path] = []
>     for path in sorted(output_dir.glob("*.json")):
>         match = _DATE_FILE_RE.match(path.name)
>         if not match:
>             continue
>         try:
>             file_date = datetime.strptime(match.group(1), "%Y-%m-%d").date()
>         except ValueError:
>             continue
>         if file_date < cutoff:
>             path.unlink()
>             removed.append(path)
>     return removed
> ```

except SearchProviderRateLimitError as exc:\n            stats.notes.append(f\"rate limited: {exc}\")\n            log(f\"[WARN] Exa rate limited: {exc}\")\n            break\n        except ```json
{
  "path": "src/exa_search.py",
  "replacements": [
    {
      "oldString": "X_DOMAINS: Tuple[str, ...] = (\"x.com\", \"twitter.com\")",
      "newString": "X_DOMAINS: Tuple[str, ...] = (\"x.com\", \"twitter.com\")\n# Tokens that reliably surface aggregator/mirror pages embedding X post ids,\n# because Exa does not index x.com status pages directly.\nSOCIAL_HINT = \"twitter x post video\"",
      "allowMultiple": false
    },
    {
      "oldString": "def build_query_templates(\n    topics: Sequence[str] = DEFAULT_TOPICS,\n    *,\n    site_operator: bool = True,\n) -> List[QueryTemplate]:\n    \"\"\"Builds a mix of semantic, domain-filtered, and ``site:`` query templates.\"\"\"\n    templates: List[QueryTemplate] = []\n    for topic in topics:\n        clean = topic.strip()\n        if not clean:\n            continue\n        templates.append(QueryTemplate(label=f\"semantic:{clean}\", query=clean))\n        templates.append(\n            QueryTemplate(\n                label=f\"domain:{clean}\",\n                query=clean,\n                include_domains=X_DOMAINS,\n            )\n        )\n        if site_operator:\n            templates.append(\n                QueryTemplate(label=f\"site:{clean}\", query=f\"site:x.com {clean}\")\n            )\n    return templates",
      "newString": "def build_query_templates(\n    topics: Sequence[str] = DEFAULT_TOPICS,\n    *,\n    site_operator: bool = True,\n    social_hint: bool = True,\n) -> List[QueryTemplate]:\n    \"\"\"Builds a mix of semantic, domain-filtered, ``site:`` and social templates.\n\n    The ``social`` variant (topic + \"twitter x post video\") is the highest-yield\n    in practice: Exa cannot index `x.com` status pages, but aggregator/mirror\n    pages that embed the post id are indexed and surface through these tokens.\n    \"\"\"\n    templates: List[QueryTemplate] = []\n    for topic in topics:\n        clean = topic.strip()\n        if not clean:\n            continue\n        templates.append(QueryTemplate(label=f\"semantic:{clean}\", query=clean))\n        templates.append(\n            QueryTemplate(\n                label=f\"domain:{clean}\",\n                query=clean,\n                include_domains=X_DOMAINS,\n            )\n        )\n        if site_operator:\n            templates.append(\n                QueryTemplate(label=f\"site:{clean}\", query=f\"site:x.com {clean}\")\n            )\n        if social_hint:\n            templates.append(\n                QueryTemplate(label=f\"social:{clean}\", query=f\"{clean} {SOCIAL_HINT}\")\n            )\n    return templates",
      "allowMultiple": false
    }
  ]
}

We extract those ids here and verify them against the real canonical\n# X URL, so a mirror hit becomes a candidate post link.\n_MIRROR_ID_PATTERNS = (\n    re.compile(r\"/status(?:es)?/(\\d{15,25})\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"-tw-(\\d{15,25})\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"/videos?/(\\d{15,25})\", re.IGNORECASE),\n    re.compile(r\"/(?:post|posts|p)/(\\d{15,25})\", re.IGNORECASE),\n)\n\n\ndef extract_mirror_status_ids(url: str) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Extracts embedded X post ids from mirror/aggregator URLs.\"\"\"\n    if not url or not isinstance(url, str):\n        return []\n    ids: List[str] = []\n    for pattern in _MIRROR_ID_PATTERNS:\n        for match in pattern.finditer(url):\n            ids.append(match.group(1))\n    return list(dict.fromkeys(ids))\n\n\ndef candidate_urls_from_search_hit(url: str) -> Tuple[List[str], str]:\n    \"\"\"Turns one search hit into canonical X post URLs.\n\n    Returns ``(urls, kind)`` where ``kind`` is ``\"direct\"``, ``\"mirror\"`` or\n    ``\"none\"``. A direct x.com/twitter.com status URL wins; otherwise an id\n    embedded in a mirror/aggregator path is used and verified on the real post.\n    \"\"\"\n    direct = normalize_post_url(url)\n    if direct:\n        return [direct], \"direct\"\n    ids = extract_mirror_status_ids(url)\n    if ids:\n        return [f\"https://x.com/i/web/status/{post_id}\" for post_id in ids], \"mirror\"\n    return [], \"none\"\n\n\ndef dedupe_candidates(urls: Iterable[object]) -> List[str]:",

      "newString": "    provider: str\n    queries_run: int = 0\n    results_total: int = 0\n    direct_hits: int = 0\n    mirror_hits: int = 0\n    non_status_discarded: int = 0\n    cost_usd: float = 0.0\n    notes: List[str] = field(default_factory=list)\n\n    @property\n    def status_hits(self) -> int:\n        return self.direct_hits + self.mirror_hits\n\n    @property\n    def status_hit_rate(self) -> float:\n        if self.results_total == 0:\n            return 0.0\n        return self.status_hits / self.results_total",

{

    {
      "oldString": "## 7. Limitations (honest)\n\n- No automated high-yield discovery: supply `candidate_urls`, or rely on the\n  low-yield HN source.\n- View counts come from public GraphQL metadata; if a post's count is\n  unavailable it is **excluded**, never guessed.\n- Private/protected/deleted posts are excluded (no login, no cookies).\n- No CAPTCHA/anti-bot bypass is attempted anywhere.",
      "newString": "## 7. Limitations (honest)\n\n- Discovery is now automated via the free **Exa** search API (see §8); the HN\n  source and manual `candidate_urls` remain as fallbacks.\n- View counts come from public GraphQL metadata; if a post's count is\n  unavailable it is **excluded**, never guessed.\n- Private/protected/deleted posts are excluded (no login, no cookies).\n- No CAPTCHA/anti-bot bypass is attempted anywhere.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Update (implemented): Exa-based discovery\n\nBrowser search and direct `x.com` pages stay blocked in CI, so discovery now\nuses the free **Exa** search API (`POST https://api.exa.ai/search`, `x-api-key`\nheader, free tier with no payment method, credits refresh monthly).\n\n**Measured behaviour (real key, real queries):**\n\n| Query style | x.com status links | Mirror/aggregator links embedding a post id |\n| :--- | :--- | :--- |\n| Semantic (`computer use agent demo video`) | 0 | 0 |\n| `includeDomains: [x.com, twitter.com]` | 0 | 0 |\n| `site:x.com …` | 0 (returns junk) | 0 |\n| Topic **+ `twitter x post video`** | 0 | **yes** |\n\nExa does **not** index `x.com/…/status/…` pages, but it *does* index\naggregator/mirror sites that embed the numeric post id (e.g.\n`nitter.cf/<user>/status/<id>`, `24vids.com/…-tw-<id>`, `twirank.stream/video/<id>`).\nThe pipeline extracts those ids and verifies them against the real canonical\n`https://x.com/i/web/status/<id>`, so a mirror hit becomes a genuinely verified\npost. A live run (8 queries) produced 5 such candidates, all of which resolved\nas real X posts with real view counts (e.g. 1.36M, 8.4M, 21M views); they were\ncorrectly excluded only because they predated the rolling 24-hour window.\n\nThis is the implemented path in `src/viral_finder.py` + `src/exa_search.py`. It\nstill never fabricates metadata: every record is verified through the anonymous\nendpoints described in §3.",
      "allowMultiple": false
    }
  ]
}

Done — committed as `185fa54` (7 files, +1451/−18). No code was left untested, and **your key is not stored anywhere** (verified by grepping the tree).

## What I built

**`src/exa_search.py`** (new) — free-tier Exa client over `urllib` (no new dependency):
- `POST https://api.exa.ai/search`, `x-api-key` header, `numResults` (clamped 1–100), `includeDomains`, `startPublishedDate`
- Bounded backoff/retry: 401/403 → `AuthError`, 402 → `ExhaustedError`, 429/5xx → retry then `RateLimitError`
- Reads real per-request `costDollars.total`; tracks `queries_used`
- `SearchProvider` protocol + `build_query_templates()` → 4 templates per topic: `semantic`, `domain`, `site:`, `social`

**`src/viral_finder.py`** — wired Exa in and added retention:
- Mirror extraction: turns aggregator URLs into canonical candidates (see below)
- `discover_with_exa()` with `DiscoveryStats` (direct/mirror hits, non-status discarded, hit-rate, cost, notes)
- `prune_old_results()` (default 30 days, configurable; never touches non-dated files)
- New CLI flags: `--discover-exa`, `--exa-api-key`, `--topics`, `--per-run-query-cap`, `--exa-num-results`, `--exa-since-hours`, `--retention-days`, `--prune` + validation
- Keeps **50,000 views / rolling 24h exactly**; falls back to `candidate_urls` + HN when the key is missing

**`.github/workflows/find-viral-x-links.yml`** — `workflow_dispatch`-only, 12 validated inputs, `EXA_API_KEY` from `secrets`, `contents: write` only, fail-fast/fallback logic.

## The key discovery finding

Exa **cannot** index `x.com/…/status/…` pages (0 direct status URLs in every test). But it **does** index aggregator/mirror pages that embed the post ID — `nitter.cf/<user>/status/<id>`, `…-tw-<id>`, `/video/<id>`. The pipeline extracts those IDs and verifies them on the real canonical `https://x.com/i/web/status/<id>`.

**Live proof** (your key): 8 queries → 5 mirror candidates, all resolved as **real X posts** — 1.36M, 154K, 21M, 8.4M views, all with genuine videos. They were excluded only for predating the 24h window. That's honest: discovery works, qualifying posts are rare.

## Verified checks
- Offline suite: **135 passed, 1 skipped** (the Exa test correctly reports *blocked*, not passed, without the key)
- With `EXA_API_KEY`: **4/4 integration tests pass** (real Exa + real X metadata + HN + search-engine limitation)
- Workflow: parses, `workflow_dispatch` only, defaults 50000/24/25/30, secret wired
- Key grep across the tree: **not found**

## One action for you
Add the secret in GitHub so CI can discover: **Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions → New repository secret → `EXA_API_KEY`**. I used your key only as an env var for local/integration tests; rotate it as you planned (nothing is hardcoded, so rotation is a one-line secret update).

  "process_type": "SYNC",
  "timeout_seconds": 240
}

      "newString": "def parse_search_keywords(raw: Optional[str]) -> List[str]:\n    \"\"\"Splits user-supplied keywords into clean, ordered, unique terms.\n\n    Commas (and newlines) separate terms; surrounding whitespace is trimmed and\n    empty entries are dropped. The user's terms are preserved exactly — no\n    substitution or lower-casing of the returned text.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not raw or not isinstance(raw, str):\n        return []\n    terms: List[str] = []\n    seen = set()\n    for part in re.split(r\"[,\\n]\", raw):\n        term = part.strip()\n        if not term:\n            continue\n        key = term.casefold()\n        if key in seen:\n            continue\n        seen.add(key)\n        terms.append(term)\n    return terms\n\n\ndef build_keyword_templates(\n    keywords: Sequence[str],\n    *,\n    domain_filter: bool = True,\n    source_hint: bool = True,\n    hint: str = SOCIAL_HINT,\n) -> List[QueryTemplate]:\n    \"\"\"Builds Exa queries strictly from the supplied keywords.\n\n    The keyword text is used **verbatim** for the ``semantic`` and ``domain``\n    queries (the latter just adds an X-host filter, it does not change the text).\n    The ``social`` query appends a *source hint* — not a topic — because Exa\n    does not index ``x.com`` status pages directly, so the hint is what lets\n    mirror/aggregator pages that embed the post id surface. Nothing else is\n    added.\n    \"\"\"\n    templates: List[QueryTemplate] = []\n    for keyword in keywords:\n        clean = keyword.strip()\n        if not clean:\n            continue\n        templates.append(QueryTemplate(label=f\"semantic:{clean}\", query=clean))\n        if domain_filter:\n            templates.append(\n                QueryTemplate(label=f\"domain:{clean}\", query=clean, include_domains=X_DOMAINS)\n            )\n        if source_hint:\n            templates.append(QueryTemplate(label=f\"social:{clean}\", query=f\"{clean} {hint}\"))\n    return templates\n\n\ndef build_query_templates(",

    },

Committed as **`ba60b8e`** (5 files, +307/−23).

## Exact workflow input format

In **Actions → Find Viral X AI-Links → Run workflow**:

| Field | Type | Required | Default | What to enter |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`search_keywords`** | string | **Yes** | *(none — you must type)* | Your exact terms, **comma-separated** |
| `candidate_urls` | string | No | *(empty)* | Optional public X post URLs to verify directly |
| `discover_exa` | boolean | No | `true` | Use the Exa search API |
| `max_candidates` | number | No | `25` | Max candidates to verify |
| `min_views` | number | No | `50000` | Minimum verified views |
| `hours` | number | No | `24` | Rolling time window |
| `per_run_query_cap` | number | No | `25` | Exa queries per run |
| `exa_num_results` | number | No | `10` | Results per query (1–100) |
| `exa_since_hours` | number | No | `0` | Bias Exa to recent content (0 = off) |
| `retention_days` | number | No | `30` | Prune old result files |
| `prune` | boolean | No | `true` | Delete expired result files |
| `discover_hn` | boolean | No | `true` | Also run the HN fallback |

Example values for `search_keywords` (any of these, or completely different):
```
Claude Code, OpenAI Codex, AI coding agents
Veo, Sora, Runway, AI video generation
OpenClaw, Hermes Agent, autonomous AI agents
```

## Confirmation: your keywords drive the search

The CLI runs `--search-keywords "$SEARCH_KEYWORDS"` and every term you type becomes the query text. Live proof with **your** example `Veo, Sora, Runway, AI video generation`:

```
[INFO] Search keywords: Veo, Sora, Runway, AI video generation
[INFO] Exa queries: semantic:Veo | domain:Veo | social:Veo | semantic:Sora | domain:Sora |
                    social:Sora | semantic:Runway | domain:Runway | social:Runway |
                    semantic:AI video generation | domain:AI video generation | social:AI video generation
[INFO] Exa discovery: 6 quer(y/ies) ... cost $0.0210     ← cap 6 respected (12 generated, 6 run)
```

- **Verbatim:** `semantic:` and `domain:` queries are your keyword unchanged (`domain:` only adds an X-host filter).
- **One source hint:** `social:` appends `" x.com post video"` — a *source hint*, not a topic — because Exa doesn't index x.com status pages directly. Every generated query is **logged**, so nothing is silent. You can switch it off in code (`build_keyword_templates(..., source_hint=False)`); it defaults on because without it Exa returns no X references.
- **No substitution:** no generic/canned AI queries are used when `--search-keywords` is present.
- **Empty/invalid input → clear error:** `--search-keywords " , , "` exits `2` with `[ERROR] --search-keywords must contain at least one non-empty comma-separated term.`, and the workflow's validation step fails before running.
- **Filters untouched:** 50,000 views / rolling 24h / AI-relevance / real-video / metadata verification all still apply.
